Bloom Energy says first power block is ready at Nebius&#8217;s 300 MW New Jersey data center William Foxley Fri, October 23, 2026 at 11:02 AM EDT 2 min read BE +7.58% NBIS +1.45% Explore stocks on Coinbase Trading disclosure Trading disclosure The above button links to Coinbase. Yahoo Finance is not a broker-dealer or investment adviser and does not offer securities or cryptocurrencies for sale or facilitate trading. Coinbase pays us for certain activity generated through this link. Prices displayed are informational. Learn more Bloom Energy (NYSE: BE) has the first fuel-cell power block for Nebius's (NASDAQ: NBIS) Vineland, New Jersey, data center ready for customer use, founder and CEO KR Sridhar said on Thursday's earnings call. Bloom reported third-quarter revenue of $1.236 billion, up 138.1% year over year. Sridhar laid out the timeline from signing to power. "We were able to get planning board approvals for our fuel cells within 3 months of signing the contract. Two months after these approvals, we have the first block of power ready for customer use," he said. The Nebius data center itself is not yet online. Sridhar said the 300 MW site will come up in phases: "They do it block by block and their first block is almost getting ready and we are already there for the first block." Stay ahead of AI infrastructure deals. Get Blockspace in your inbox. Nebius and Bloom signed the agreement on May 14. Vineland's Planning Board approved developer DataOne's 587,980-square-foot Phase 2 expansion on August 17, including multiple fuel-cell areas and an LNG tank. The fuel cells replaced an earlier plan for 32 natural-gas engines, which drew resident objections over emissions. The contract covers about 250 MW of guaranteed capacity and about 328 MW of installed capacity, according to a Nebius filing. It runs in three expected phases, each with a 10-year supply term. Monthly service fees total up to $2.6 billion over the term, with Bloom installing, operating and maintaining the systems. Bloom says the project investment for Nebius stands at $1.7 billion. Industrial Development Funding is lead developer and Oaktree Capital Management is supplying infrastructure capital. Morgan Stanley structured the tax-equity financing and is the only tax-equity investor while MUFG Bank led the senior debt. Nebius' Vineland campus sits under its multiyear agreement with Microsoft. "We will not be the bottleneck to them turning on their data center their entire 300 megawatts," Sridhar said. Stay ahead of AI infrastructure deals. Get Blockspace in your inbox. Bloom lifted its full-year 2026 revenue guidance, setting the range at $4.4 billion to $4.6 billion, and raised its non-GAAP EPS guidance to $3.10 to $3.40. {"contentId":"caca960f-7eb1-345f-80ce-ebe897b98d5a","canonicalUrl":"https://blockspace.media/insight/bloom-energy-readies-nebius-power/","owningPropertyUrl":"https://finance.yahoo.com/energy/articles/bloom-energy-says-first-power-150207042.html","providerId":"blockspace_702","commerceSiteId":"","site":"finance","region":"US","lang":"en-US","tags":[{"value":"yfinance:region=us","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"yfrontpage:region=us","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:category=fin_cat_crypto","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:category=fin_tier3","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:category=fin_whitelistedproviders","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:hosted=yes","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:provider_category=DEFAULT","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:provider_id=blockspace_702","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:category=100000002","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"},{"value":"ymedia:category=100000008","property":null,"uuid":null,"type":"system"}],"categories":[],"commerceArticleType":""} View Comments Terms and Privacy Policy Your Privacy Choices More Info Recommended Stories Copyright © 2026 Yahoo.
